| S2-07 | major | Resolved, ADR-025: largest fitting prefix, essential first; protection limited to the fetched set | `R-SYN-11: a catalog larger than the budget syncs its largest fitting prefix essential-first` |
| S2-08 | major | Resolved, §7.10: no unknown-size estimate in the plan (`PlanTotals.fetchBytes` counts known sizes); per-fetch ledger | `R-SYN-11: a manifest without byteSize never fails the space check with insufficientSpace` |
| S2-09 | major | Resolved, §7.10: committed bytes stay in the ledger until the next free-space read | `R-SYN-11: committed bytes stay in the headroom ledger so the reserve is never crossed` — not written at the level-4 merge (Stage 3, ADR-053); the bytes in flight are covered by `R-SYN-11: ensureAvailable whose member lacks room beside a transfer in flight fails with insufficientSpace` *(exists)* |
| S2-10 | major | Resolved, §7.4: the pipeline cap gates non-user lanes only | `R-SYN-3: a user-initiated job starts while bulk work fills the verify and commit pipeline` — not written at the level-4 merge (Stage 3, ADR-053) |
| S2-11 | major | Resolved, ADR-029 | `R-STO-11: a resolve during migration adopts that legacy file first and never waits for the whole catalog` |
| S2-12 | major | Resolved, ADR-016, §5: `InstallAck.retired` releases the old snapshot off main (implemented); bulk and prefetch installs ≤ 10 Hz through `publish(…, urgency:)` and `StoreConfiguration.snapshotInstallInterval` with the trailing install on the store's deadline (round 3); the store's present table is maintained incrementally | `P-8: UI-facing calls make zero main-thread file-system calls and each stays within ui_call_ms_max` (G, §11 P-8, Level 5) *(exists; ADR-054)*; `R-STO-13: bulk publishes install at most one snapshot per snapshotInstallInterval and a user-initiated publish installs at once` |
| S2-13 | major | Resolved, ADR-037: bundle index built off main by `AssetFallbackChain.prepare` (implemented) | `P-8: the fallback chain's bundle step does no work on the main thread` (G, §11 P-8, assigned at the level-4 merge, ADR-053) *(exists; ADR-054)* |
| S2-14 | major | Resolved, §11 note: benchmarks run serialized under the parent suite `ZTronAssetKitBenchmarks` (declared in round 3; per-WP nested extension suites); the P-9 heartbeat is a cooperative-pool task timed with `LiveTimeSource` (benchmarks are exempt from Q-4's no-sleep rule, which binds the deterministic suite) | `P-9: 64 concurrent resolves plus hashing keep the heartbeat gap p99 within budget` (G, §11 P-9, Level 5) *(exists; ADR-054)* |
| S2-15 | major | Resolved, ADR-014 (as store-2 S2-16) | `R-ENV-1: a cancelled ManualTimeSource sleeper throws promptly and leaves no sleeper behind` *(exists)* |
| S2-16 | major | Resolved, ADR-024 (implemented in `Backoff.delay`) | `R-SYN-5: backoff delays are full jitter keyed by asset and attempt and reproducible from the seed` *(exists)* |
| S2-17 | major | Resolved, ADR-023, §7.9: bulk outcomes folded into run events; pruned subjects retired; registration on first pull | `R-SYN-12: a stalled subscriber's backlog never exceeds one slot per progress key and subject during a 10,000-asset sync` |
| S2-18 | major | Resolved, §8: no URLSession in `AssetClient.init`; the source is built by `RemoteSourceFactory` in `start()` (implemented) | `P-12: AssetClient.init completes within init_ms_max and performs no file-system call` (run with `.live()` as well as fakes; G, §11 P-12, Level 5) *(exists; ADR-054)* |
| S2-19 | major | Resolved, ADR-021: no control stream exists | `R-SYN-14: no task outlives a transfer in the live foreground backend` — not written at the level-4 merge (Stage 3, ADR-053) |

### 12.5 transport-1 and transport-2

| ID | Sev | Resolution | Regression test |
|---|---|---|---|
| TR1-1 | critical | Resolved, ADR-020, ADR-013, §4.3: single-flight per content, per-attempt names, exclusive renames, `.verifying` claim | `R-SYN-4: two identifiers with the same checksum share one transfer and both become present`; `R-STO-1: partial files, continuations and adopted downloads land in staging under their exclusive names` (B) *(exists)*, `R-STO-3: bytes appended to a staged file after publication begins are never published` (B) *(exists)* and `R-INT-1: a cancelled hash whose claim cannot be handed back discards the claimed file and keeps the producer's new .part` (B) *(exists)* (exclusive per-attempt names and the `.verifying` claim; re-pointed at the level-4 merge, ADR-053) |
| TR1-2 | major | Resolved, ADR-019: data tasks append into staging | `R-STO-1: a cancelled, paused or failed foreground transfer leaves no byte outside the store root` |
| TR1-3 | major | Resolved, §6.3: checksum-less transfers are never resumed; every staged file is hashed and the digest recorded (R-INT-6) | `R-NET-8: checksum-less transfers are never resumed`; background: `R-NET-8: a checksum-less background transfer is never resumed: its pause keeps no resume data` (D; ADR-050) |
| TR1-4 | major | Resolved, ADR-019, §6.3: no URLSession resume data on the foreground path; −1002/416/a 206 that cannot continue the partial → `resumeRejected`, partial discarded, fresh attempt; a changed validator yields a 200 that restarts from zero (ADR-050) | `R-NET-8: a refused or invalid resume discards the partial and restarts from zero in the next attempt` (F); transport half: `R-NET-8: a refused or invalid resume yields resumeRejected and a fresh attempt stages the whole body` (D) (ADR-050) |
| TR1-5 | major | Resolved, ADR-019: sidecars hold only a validator; a resumed request is rebuilt with the current key | `R-NET-8: a resumed request carries the current key, never a stale one` |
| TR1-6 | major | Resolved, ADR-031 (`ForbiddenEvidence.keyPresented`, `PolicyViolation.keyWithheldOnRedirect` in the Model) | `R-NET-6: a 403 after the key was withheld on a cross-origin redirect is reported as keyWithheldOnRedirect` |
| TR1-7 | major | Resolved: `ForbiddenEvidence.hint` checks body shape before purpose (implemented) | `R-NET-6: every TransferError maps to one failure class and the documented AssetError` *(exists)*; `R-NET-6: an XML 403 on the manifest is object-missing-likely, never key rejection` |
| TR1-8 | major | Resolved: `SizeGuard` aborts on `byteSize` and treats −1 as unknown; written bytes measured in staging (implemented) | `R-NET-7: the size guard aborts when the written or announced size exceeds the cap or byteSize is exceeded` *(exists)* |
| T2-01 | critical | Resolved, ADR-019. The ~794 MiB of `CFNetworkDownload_*.tmp` left by earlier probes are outside the repository and must be removed by the human (§15) | `R-STO-1: a cancelled, paused or failed foreground transfer leaves no byte outside the store root` |
| T2-02 | critical | Resolved, ADR-013, §4.3 (as TR1-1) | `R-STO-1: partial files, continuations and adopted downloads land in staging under their exclusive names` (B) *(exists)*, `R-STO-3: bytes appended to a staged file after publication begins are never published` (B) *(exists)* and `R-INT-1: a cancelled hash whose claim cannot be handed back discards the claimed file and keeps the producer's new .part` (B) *(exists)* (exclusive per-attempt names and the `.verifying` claim; re-pointed at the level-4 merge, ADR-053) |
| T2-03 | critical | Resolved, ADR-028: session → tasks → staging → reconcile → GC; `wanted` = applied entries not present; the fake driver delivers completions only on `activate()` | `R-NET-3: a download completed while the app was dead is adopted from staging, not re-downloaded`; `R-NET-3: an offline launch cancels no live background task` |
| T2-04 | major | Resolved, ADR-039, `RemoteSource.invalidate` contract: the foreground session returns after its delegate's last callback; invalidate before close. Narrowed in round 2: the background session is released without waiting, its late staging files are reconciled at the next launch, and `StagingSink` promises nothing after close (`StagingError.closed` removed) | `R-SYN-15: no foreground staging write lands after the store is closed` |
| T2-05 | major | Resolved, §6.4: the driver is decision-free (`cancel` returns the resume data; the coordinator records the pause intent); the kept data (checksummed content, newest `maxTasks`) is continued once by the engine's restart of that content (ruling D-14.2; ADR-050) | `R-NET-3: a background pause is reported paused and keeps its resume data`; `R-NET-8: a paused background transfer restarted by the engine continues from the kept resume data, once` (D) |
| T2-06 | major | Resolved, ADR-013: `TransferAttempt(epoch, sequence)` with a persisted epoch | `R-SYN-1: identifiers, transfers, attempts, staging names, extensions and levels order deterministically` (A) *(exists; attempts order by epoch, then sequence)*; the cross-launch collision case (a persisted epoch unique per launch) is not written at the level-4 merge (Stage 3, ADR-053) |
| T2-07 | major | Resolved (as TR1-4) | `R-NET-8: a refused or invalid resume discards the partial and restarts from zero in the next attempt` (F); transport half: `R-NET-8: a refused or invalid resume yields resumeRejected and a fresh attempt stages the whole body` (D) (ADR-050) |
| T2-08 | major | Resolved, ADR-021: a restart is a new attempt with a new handle | `R-NET-8: an invalid resume yields resumeRejected and the engine's next attempt starts fresh` (F; ADR-050) |
| T2-09 | major | Resolved, ADR-026: `.acceptedByOwner` covers the key, the hop limit and the size cap (documented on `KeyRedirectExposure`) | `R-NET-3: a key-bearing transfer never uses the background session unless the owner accepted redirect exposure` |
| T2-10 | major | Resolved, §2: the coordinator's pump exists only while a background transfer is outstanding | `R-NET-3: the coordinator owns a pump task only while a background transfer is outstanding` (D) *(exists)*; `R-SYN-14: after a sync quiesces the engine owns zero timers and zero tasks and observes nothing` *(exists)* (re-pointed at the level-4 merge, ADR-053) |
| T2-11 | major | Resolved, ADR-028: the background session is created only by `start()` (or by background-event handling, which starts the client first) after the store is open; a background launch runs no sync unless one is due | `R-NET-3: handling background events before start opens the store first and runs no sync` (G, §11 R-NET-3, assigned at the level-4 merge, ADR-053) *(exists; ADR-054)* |
| T2-12 | major | Resolved, ADR-032 (`TransientCause.sourceVersionSkew` in the Model) | `R-APL-4: a catalog and pack version skew is transient and never quarantines the asset` |
| T2-13 | major | Resolved, ADR-027: keep-scope exclusions (implemented in `PackReleasePlanner`) fed by the retention scope (`AssetClientConfiguration.retentionScope`, `setRetentionScope(_:)`, round 2), `StorageReport.sourceCopyBytes` fed by `RemoteSource.sourceCopyBytes()` (round 2), eviction ranking | `R-CLN-10: a media pack is released only after every catalog entry in it is adopted or excluded` *(exists)*; `R-CLN-10: entries outside the retention scope never block a pack release`; `R-CLN-9: storageReport counts bytes the OS still holds in adopted packs` |
| T2-14 | major | Resolved, §6.6: the update check is scheduled, never awaited before reading the installed catalog; pack availability is single-flight in an owned task | `R-APL-4: a hanging update check never delays reading the installed catalog` |
| T2-15 | major | Resolved, §11: the P-11 benchmark syncs through `ensureAvailable`, whose user-initiated batch lane may use every slot, reserved one included; the final commit flushes as soon as the P-5 window allows (§4.1) | `P-11: assets with server latency sync at least speedup_min times faster at the configured concurrency than at 1` |

### 12.6 Minor findings

| ID | Disposition |
|---|---|
| store-1 S1-14 | Accepted (ADR-015): ordered commits barrier-sync the temp file; `.full` only for durable commits; the I-5 checker states its model assumption. |
| store-1 S1-15 | Accepted: the `fsync` fallback applies only to ENOTSUP/EINVAL/ENOTTY (`LiveFileSystem` doc); table test `R-STO-6: every errno and Cocoa error maps to the closed FileSystemError taxonomy`. |
| store-1 S1-16 | Accepted (§4.1): generations allocated at capture; the auditor decodes the committed index. |
| store-1 S1-17 | Accepted (ADR-028): unpaired `.resume` files survive `stagingClaimGrace`; staging bytes count in the reserve check and `StorageReport.stagingBytes`. |
| store-1 S1-18 | Accepted (§4.7, ADR-029): non-following listing; EEXIST in phase 2 is dedupe after the existing blob is known durable; no snapshot before reconciliation; the EXDEV copy is hashed while copying. |
| store-2 S2-17 | Accepted (as store-1 S1-04). |
| store-2 S2-18 | Accepted: `makeRoom` continues past a newly leased candidate to the next LRU candidates, or rolls the marks back. |
| store-2 S2-19 | Accepted: access stamps are a catalog-bounded map delivered in `InstallAck.accesses`; future times clamped (`recordAccesses` doc). |
| store-2 S2-20 | Accepted (`AssetStore.purge` doc): terminates when each selected key is reaped, rescued or deferred by a lease; phase checked after every await. |
| store-2 S2-21 | Accepted (§4.7): vacuous truth excluded (no entries ⇒ stamp not migrated), `inProgress` resumes, and an overlap of legacy root and store root is refused at configuration. |
| store-2 S2-22 | Accepted (ADR-018): quarantine dooms as `corrupt`, never rescued. |
| store-2 S2-23 | Accepted (§4.5): a size contradiction dooms the name as `corrupt`; 7c skips it. |
| store-2 S2-24 | Accepted (§4.3): checksum-less publishes commit before returning. |
| store-2 S2-25 | Accepted (ADR-015 as amended by ADR-050): one retry after freeing `index.tmp`, and `index.prev` only while `index.bin` is the file known to hold the committed generation (B-28); the scheduled retry backs off exponentially to 5 minutes on the engine's alarm (B-29); `requireCommitted` throws `.fileSystem(.noSpace)` (mapped to `insufficientSpace`). The store never produces `degradedReadOnly`. |
| store-2 S2-26 | Accepted: live file-system tests and benchmarks use a per-test directory inside this repository, never the system temp directory: the storage tests and benchmarks under `.build-tests/` at the repository root, the other suites under their own `.build-*` directories (`.build-transport-fixtures`, `.build-testsupport-fixtures`), all git-ignored through `.build*` (ADR-050); `LiveLegacyDefaults` and `LiveFileSystem.standardDirectory` are the deliberately untested seams (§14). |
| store-2 S2-27 | Accepted: a P-13 "batch" is one deletion unit: at most 64 unlinks and at most `BlobDeletionUnit.budget` (B-10); P-13 asserts the maximum unit duration (ADR-050; the owner rules on the proposal there); deletable keys are kept in an ordered set so selection is O(batch). |
| sync-1 S1-16 | Accepted (§7.4): `pump()` loops after marking a victim. |
| sync-1 S1-17 | Accepted: drivers are spawned with the job's lane priority (`TaskPriority` mapped from `AssetPriority`) and promotion does not need to raise it (the transport's priority is the effective lever). |
| sync-1 S1-18 | Declined: a `cancel()` racing the registration hop may still start one request; the transfer is cancelled one actor turn later, which costs at most one request per swipe. Fixing it needs synchronous engine registration, i.e. a lock (`@unchecked`) the budget forbids. Measured cost is bounded and recorded for P-10. |
| sync-1 S1-19 | Accepted (ADR-023): progress keys retire with their transfer; subscribers are dropped at shutdown after `clientShutDown`, so `subscribers == 0` holds. |
| sync-2 S2-20 | Accepted (§7.4): `user.batch` preempts at most one transfer per batch and never starves prefetch beyond one slot. |
| sync-2 S2-21 | Accepted (§7.7): a cached 304 never resets the trip count. |
| sync-2 S2-22 | Accepted: the negative cache is LRU-bounded (`unknownIDCacheCapacity`), throttle keys retire with transfers, lane FIFOs compact. |
| sync-2 S2-23 | Accepted: `CapacityPurpose.opportunistic` for bulk and prefetch, `.important` for user work (`FileSystem.availableCapacity(at:for:)`). |
| sync-2 S2-24 | Accepted (§7.9): a trailing progress emission after a quiet period. |
| sync-2 S2-25 | Accepted: thermal hysteresis (`PowerPolicy.resumeBackgroundAtThermal`) and run-restart debounce (`SyncTuning.pendingRunDebounce`). |
| sync-2 S2-26 | Accepted (§14): one post-await revalidation funnel, the planner injectable (`SyncPlanning`, `SyncEngine.init(store:environment:configuration:planner:)`, added in round 2), illegal transitions counted rather than trapped in release. |
| sync-2 S2-27 | Accepted: `ManualTimeSource.pendingSleepers`, `nextDeadline`, `waitForSleepers(atLeast:)` exist (implemented). |
| sync-2 S2-28 | Accepted (§7.10, `AssetStore.admit` doc): point admission never stores an ETag. |
| sync-2 S2-29 | Accepted (§7.4): `interactiveConcurrencyAtCritical` enforced; `popValid(fitting:)` returns the first valid job whose known size fits the ledger, unknown sizes always fit. |
| transport-1 TR1-9 | Moot on the foreground path under ADR-019 (no archived requests: a resume is a new request that `RequestFactory` builds from the sidecar after the policy check). On the background path no check reads the archived request, and R-NET-4 holds without one (ADR-050): the coordinator checks the job's URL against `TransportSecurityPolicy` before every start, a continuation of resume data included; that data lives only in memory, in the coordinator that kept it, whose policy and key never change (a key or configuration change builds a new source and coordinator, whose table starts empty; ADR-026's amendment), so the allow-list cannot shrink between the pause and the continuation; a download whose final origin differs from its original one is refused and never adopted (`redirectRefused(.unenforceableInBackground)`); and under the default `.refuse` no background request carries the key. |
| transport-1 TR1-10 | Accepted (D-6; ADR-050): the request factory joins `baseURL` and a validated `remotePath` by percent-encoded path components only (`appendingPathComponent` per `/`-separated component, as the prior art), rejecting empty components, `.`/`..` (also percent-encoded), `?`, `#` and `\` (`PolicyViolation.invalidURL`); the configured `manifestPath` goes through the same join; a manifest listing already percent-encoded paths is double-encoded, exactly as in the prior art. |
| transport-1 TR1-11 | Accepted (ADR-031): 401/−1012/−1013 are `transient(.authenticationChallenge)`. |
| transport-1 TR1-12 | Accepted (implemented): `PackID` rejects `.` and `..`; `PackLocation` validates every component (`R-SEC-2: pack locations reject traversal, empty and dot segments and invalid pack ids` *(exists)*). |
| transport-1 TR1-13 | Accepted: allowed hosts are trailing-dot-stripped (implemented in `TransportSecurityPolicy`); oversize is classified before pause; one sidecar write per pause; foreign staging names are collected. |
| transport-2 T2-16 | Accepted (implemented): `BackgroundEventsRelay` and `SnapshotMirror` have `nonisolated init`. |
| transport-2 T2-17 | Accepted: the background task cap counts re-attached tasks and admitted starts still on their way to the OS, and is exact under concurrency (`startIfAdmitted` checks and reserves in one actor turn, D-8; `R-NET-3: concurrent transfers never take more background tasks than the cap`; ADR-050); a handler is completed even when its events overflowed (completion after the staged-file reconcile). |
| transport-2 T2-18 | Accepted: Apple-tier writes go through `.parttmp` then an exclusive rename; `.resumetmp` is a parsed kind collected by GC (ADR-013). |
| transport-2 T2-19 | Accepted (implemented): `ForegroundTransferBackend.deinit` invalidates its session. |
| transport-2 T2-20 | Accepted: the catalog digest runs on the bulk executor; `ManifestFetchResult.notModified(removedSources:)` keeps removals (implemented in the Model). |
| transport-2 T2-21 | Accepted: `taskDescription` persistence across relaunch is listed as unverified (DEVICE-TEST-PLAN); there is no `guard`-line coverage device (the tier check is plain code whose `osSupportsAppleTier == false` decision is covered through `TierSelection.select`). |

## 13. Review dispositions

### 13.1 Contract-fix round 1

The independent review of the Stage 1 contracts raised 20 issues (11 blocking). Each is fixed as described, or partly declined with the reason given.

| # | Issue | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Build: targets declared without sources; staged deletions of the skeleton | **Fixed.** Contract sources for all 7 targets; 8 test targets with behaviour tests (37 in the normal run plus 1 in the benchmark target; none assertion-free); debug, release and iOS builds clean under `-warnings-as-errors`; `--quick` green. The git index still records the skeleton deletions; the orchestrator commits. |
| 2 | `TransferPurpose` undeclared | **Fixed.** `Sources/ZTronAssetModel/Transfer/TransferPurpose.swift`, as transport.md:246 specifies, documented. |
| 3 | `ARCHITECTURE.md` missing | **Fixed.** This document: §11 covers all 104 IDs with target, WP and test; §9 one section per H-n with decisions and exact test names; §10 the ledger; §3 every file on disk; §7.1 the state machine; §7.5 the priority table; §7.6 the error-to-action table; §7, §7.3, §7.5, §7.6, §7.7 and §3, the sections the code cites, exist. |
| 4 | ADRs cited but missing | **Fixed.** ADR-012…041, including every cited number (013, 017, 019, 020, 024, 025, 026, 027, 035, 036), NEEDS-HUMAN-REVIEW on layout, security, retention, defaults, shims and facade deviations. Storage location (SPEC §4.4) stays ADR-001; its numeric defaults are ADR-035. The R-CAR-6 shim location is ADR-033. Provisional design ADR numbers are mapped in DECISIONS.md. |
| 5 | Model types missing | **Fixed.** `Transfer/` (`RemoteSource`, `RemoteSourceFactory`, `TransferBackend`, `TransferHandle`, `TransferUpdate`, `TransferControl`, `TransferRequest`, `TransferJob`, `ManifestRequest`, `ManifestFetchResult`, `ETag`, `StagedFile`, `ReattachedTransfer`, `ResumeSidecar`, `ResumeData`, `StagingSink`), `Environment/` (`AssetEnvironment`, `BlockingExecutor` + `DispatchBlockingExecutor`, `FileSystem`, `LegacyDefaults`, `AppVersionSource`, `LogTap`, `MemoryPressureSource`), `Events/` (`AssetEvent`, `SyncProgress`, `TransferProgress`, `RetryDisposition`, `BlockReason`, `CircuitState`), `Reports/` (`AvailabilityReport`, `StorageReport`, `SyncReport`, `SyncOutcome`, `MetricsSnapshot`, `PurgeRequest`, `PurgeResult`, `AssetLocation`, `SyncStamp`, `PlanTotals`), `Tier/` (`TierInputs` with `osSupportsAppleTier`, `TierSelection`). |
| 6 | Storage contract missing | **Fixed, with one placement deviation.** `AssetStore` actor; `AssetSnapshot`, `SnapshotEntry`, `SnapshotKey`; `AssetRecord`, `Residency`, `DoomReason`; `StoreError` with its `AssetError` mapping; `SnapshotSink` + `InstallAck` (the acknowledgement proves what is installed); leases; `StoreStagingSink`; `LiveFileSystem`; `LiveLegacyDefaults`. **Deviation:** the `FileSystem` protocol, `FileSystemError` and `LegacyDefaults` are declared in the Model (ADR-038) because the single `AssetEnvironment` (SPEC §4.2) must hold them; their live conformers are in Storage, the only code that touches POSIX, `FileManager` or `UserDefaults`. Fault injection, the deterministic crash-point sequence, calling-thread recording and attribute recording are on `FakeFileSystem` (TestSupport); the free-space query is `availableCapacity(at:for:)`. |
| 7 | Sync contract missing | **Fixed, with one placement deviation.** `SyncPlanner.plan(manifest:snapshot:scope:policy:freeSpace:)`, `SyncPlan`, `PlannedFetch`, `ReconcileStep`, `SpaceAssessment`, `SyncEngine` (`awaitAsset`, `registerGroup`/`releaseGroup`, runs, introspection), `EngineIntrospection` (`package`, readable by benchmarks), `RefreshDecision`, `JobState`/`JobStateKind`, `CircuitBreaker`, `Backoff`, `LaneAdmission`, `TransferReconciler`. **Deviation:** `SyncStamp` is in the Model, because the store persists it inside the index (R-STO-2) and Storage cannot see Sync. |
| 8 | Transport, AppleHosted, Kit contracts missing | **Fixed.** Transport: `HTTPRemoteSource`, `SessionDelegate` (`Sendable` with `let`s only, no escape), `TaskMailbox`, `ForegroundTransferBackend`, `LiveDeviceConditionsSource`, `BackgroundSessionDriver` (decision-free) and `BackgroundSessionBackend.swift`, `BackgroundEventsRelay`, `TransportSecurityPolicy`, `RedirectPolicy`, `SizeGuard`, `TransferClassifier`. AppleHosted: `PackBackend`, `PackFileReader`, `PackID`, `PackLocation`, `PackError`, `PackReleasePlanner`, `AppleHostedRemoteSource` (`@available(iOS 26.0, macOS 26.0, *)` inside `#if canImport(BackgroundAssets)`), `LiveAssetPackBackend.swift`, `AppleHostedTier` (the single `#available`). Kit: `AssetClient`, `AssetClientConfiguration`, `SnapshotMirror`, `PrefetchHandle`, `PrefetchWindow`, `PrefetchWindowController`, `BundleLookup`, `AssetFallbackChain`, `handleBackgroundEvents`, `diagnosticReport`, `AssetEnvironment.live`. No URLSession is created in `AssetClient.init`. |
| 9 | TestSupport fakes missing | **Fixed.** All declared; `ManualTimeSource` (with `pendingSleepers`, `nextDeadline`, `wakeLog`, `waitForSleepers`, `sleepDevice`), `InlineBlockingExecutor`, `SeededRandom`, `FakeDeviceConditionsSource` (actor), `FakeLegacyDefaults`, `RecordingLogTap`, `FakeTransferHandle`, `FakeAppVersionSource`, `FakeRemoteSourceFactory` implemented; the rest are interfaces for WP-H. 0 escapes; Mutex fakes gated, tests gated per `@Test` (verified). |
| 10 | 86 critical/major review findings without resolution | **Fixed.** §12: every one resolved with its ADR or section and a named regression test; none rejected; minors dispositioned. |
| 11 | R-CAR-6 shims have no target | **Fixed.** `ZTronAssetCompatibilityTests` added to Package.swift (ADR-033) with `Shims/` (`CDNAsset`, `CDNSyncProgress`, `CDNRefreshPolicy`, `CDNProviding`) and three R-CAR-6 tests; the adapters over `AssetClient` are WP-G's in the same target. |
| 12 | `@available` on suites rejected | **Fixed.** ADR-014 records per-`@Test` gating (single-platform `@available(macOS 15.0, *)`), verified by `SyncContractTests`, `AppleHostedContractTests` and `KitContractTests`. Superseded by ADR-051: every gate names both platforms (`@available(macOS 15.0, iOS 18.0, *)`), since a macOS-only gate vanishes on iOS and the gated tests failed to build for the simulator. |
| 13 | APPLE-API-VERIFICATION §3.2 inaccurate | **Fixed.** §3.2 and §0 Q3 corrected with build-manifest evidence and an ungated `.timeLimit` test (ADR-041); the macOS floor stays 12. |
| 14 | R-MAN-5 diff vs single-flight key | **Fixed.** `byteSize` dropped from `ContentIdentity.unverified` (ADR-034); test `R-MAN-5: a checksum-less entry changes content identity only when its remotePath changes`. |
| 15 | `MonotonicInstant.adding` | **Fixed.** `+infinity` saturates to `UInt64.max`; the headroom check runs in the integer domain with `addingReportingOverflow`; tested. |
| 16 | `ManifestDiff` cannot be applied; `change(of:)` O(n) | **Fixed in part.** The diff now carries `targetOrder` and `upserts` and offers `applied(to:)`; the R-MAN-4 property is tested. **Declined:** an index of the classes, because the five class lists are public mutable arrays and a second structure would have to be kept in sync by every mutator; `change(of:)` is documented as diagnostics and tests only. |
| 17 | `KeyRedirectExposure.acceptedByOwner` violates R-NET-4/R-NET-7 by opt-in | **Fixed as an owner decision.** ADR-026 (NEEDS-HUMAN-REVIEW) states the trade-off for the key, the hop limit and the size cap; the default stays `.refuse` and R-NET-4 tests cover it. The case is kept until the owner decides. |
| 18 | `CFNetworkDownload_*.tmp` outside the repository | **Fixed in the design** (ADR-019: data tasks; loopback tests and benchmarks use only the data-task path). **Not deleted:** the files are outside the repository; §15 gives the human the cleanup command. |
| 19 | Memory probes vs the unsafe budget | **Fixed.** ADR-040 and the `QualityBudgets.swift` doc comment: memory helpers live in `Tests/ZTronAssetKitBenchmarks`, never in `Sources/`. |
| 20 | Target dependencies of TestSupport | **Fixed.** `RemoteSource`, `TransferBackend` and `StagingSink` are in the Model; `FakePackBackend` is in `Tests/ZTronAssetAppleHostedTests`, `FakeBackgroundSessionDriver` in `Tests/ZTronAssetTransportTests` (ADR-040); TestSupport keeps its declared dependencies. |

Further amendments made while resolving the reviews: `TimeSource.uptimeNow()` (ADR-017), `InstallAck.retired` (sync-2 S2-12), `RemoteSource.handleBackgroundEvents(identifier:)` (R-NET-3 completion protocol), `ManifestFetchResult.notModified(removedSources:)` (transport-2 T2-20), `StagingFileName` is `Codable` (sidecars).

### 13.2 Contract-fix round 2

The second independent review of the Stage 1 contracts raised 12 issues (2 blocking). All 12 are fixed; nothing is declined. Where a fix deviates from the reviewer's suggested shape, the reason is given. ADRs: ADR-042…045 (new), ADR-014, ADR-027, ADR-038, ADR-039 (amended).

| # | Issue | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Blocking.** R-SYN-15: APIs non-throwing after shutdown | **Fixed, option (a)** (ADR-042, NEEDS-HUMAN-REVIEW for the signatures). Facade: `start`, `availability`, `subscribe`, `isSyncDue`, `lastSyncedAt`, `storageReport`, `performMaintenance` (now returns `MaintenanceReport`), `reportUnreadable`, `metrics`, `diagnosticReport` are `async throws(AssetError)`; the engine methods return `Result<_, AssetError>` (`registerGroup`, `subscribe`, `backgroundSessionEventsPending` too). Shapes that cannot throw fail in their typed way: `sync` → `SyncOutcome.failed(.shutDown)` (kept non-throwing because its outcome already carries `AssetError`; a second channel would duplicate it); `prefetch` stays synchronous (P-8) and the handle gained `awaitRegistration() throws(AssetError)`; a new `events` stream yields `clientShutDown` and finishes; `lookup` returns `nil`; `register(_:)` throws. Releases and `shutdown()` stay idempotent. Tests: `R-SYN-15: every API called after shutdown fails with shutDown` (planned, §11) and `R-SYN-15: a prefetch handle made after shutdown reports shutDown and holds nothing` *(exists)*. |
| 2 | **Blocking.** `.stage(Data)` cannot reach the store's staging sink | **Fixed as suggested** (ADR-045): `StagingAttachable` (ungated) in TestSupport, `FakeRemoteSource` conforms (`attach(staging:)`, `attachedStaging`), `FakeRemoteSourceFactory.makeRemoteSource` attaches before returning (a cast, no `#available`). `.stage(Data)` is specified to write through the sink (`createPartial`, `appendPartial`) and report `.staged`; without a sink it fails with `transient(.localIO)`. Test `R-NET-1: FakeRemoteSourceFactory hands the store's staging sink to an attachable source before returning it` *(exists)*. |
| 3 | `PrefetchHandle` re-lane: deinit releases before the successor registers (S1-05 regression) | **Fixed; failing test first.** `cancel(after:)` now runs its ordered release in a task that holds `self`, so the deinit release runs after it and is a no-op (the registry ignores released tokens, which are never reused). `PrefetchRegistry` (Kit, `SyncEngine` conforms) makes the handle testable with a slow-registration fake. `R-CAR-3: a re-laned prefetch handle releases its old interest only after the successor registered` recorded 4 issues against the round-1 logic (`released != nil`, holders, log order, `minimumHolders == ["a": 1]`) and passes on the fix; `R-CAR-4` deinit and early-cancel tests added. |
| 4 | `StorageReport.sourceCopyBytes` unreachable from Sync | **Fixed as suggested:** `RemoteSource.sourceCopyBytes() async -> Int64`; `HTTPRemoteSource` returns 0 (implemented); `AppleHostedRemoteSource` sums `PackBackend.installedBytes` of adopted, unreleased packs (WP-E stub; the merged sum is §6.6's, ADR-050); `FakeRemoteSource.setSourceCopyBytes(_:)` (implemented); `SyncEngine.storageReport()` documents the fill (RE-S32). |
| 5 | No atomic durable adoption report | **Fixed** with a narrower signature: `AssetStore.adoptionReport() async throws(StoreError) -> AdoptionReport`. The exclusions come from the store's retention scope (issue 6) instead of an `excluding:` argument, so eviction and release read one source of truth. §4.6a, §6.6, REENTRANCY R15 and RE-S34. |
| 6 | No keep/retention scope | **Fixed with both suggested forms:** `AssetClientConfiguration.retentionScope` (default `.all`) and `EngineConfiguration.retentionScope` for launch, plus `AssetClient.setRetentionScope(_:)` / `SyncEngine.setRetentionScope(_:)` / `AssetStore.setRetentionScope(_:)` for paywall changes at run time (a configuration value alone could not follow a purchase). It scopes bulk runs, orders eviction and feeds `excludedRemotePaths`; it deletes and fetches nothing. Recorded in ADR-027. |
| 7 | `StagingSink` `.closed` unimplementable; background invalidate waits for hours | **Fixed as suggested:** the `.closed` promise **and** the then-unproduced `StagingError.closed` case are removed; `StagingSink` documents the T2-04 ordering instead. `RemoteSource.invalidate` waits only for the foreground delegate (and the Apple source's `writeStaged`); the background session is released without waiting and late files are reconciled at the next launch (ADR-028, ADR-039). The test is narrowed to `R-SYN-15: no foreground staging write lands after the store is closed`. |
| 8 | Seams for H-5, snapshot acks, census waits, planner | **Fixed as suggested** (ADR-045), and implemented with tests: `SteppedBlockingExecutor` (actor), `ScriptedSnapshotSink` (package actor), `FakeDeviceConditionsSource.waitForObservers(equalTo:)`, plus `FakeMemoryPressureSource`; `SyncPlanning`/`StandardSyncPlanner` and `SyncEngine.init(store:environment:configuration:planner:)`. |
| 9 | R-CLN-11, pins, diagnostics have no entry point | **Fixed** (ADR-044): `@MainActor register(_:) throws(AssetError)` / `unregister(_:)` over `PurgeableCacheRelay` (weak, epoch-ordered; implemented and tested) and `SyncEngine.observeMemoryPressure(epoch:handler:)` (one owned observer only while a live cache is registered); `pin(_:)` / `unpin(_:)` through `SyncEngine.setPinned(_:pinned:)`; `SyncEngine.diagnostics() -> Result<EngineDiagnostics, AssetError>` (async, because it reads the store's generation and stamp). `DiagnosticError` moved to the Model. |
| 10 | `AssetLog` unreachable below the facade; `LogLine` has no subsystem | **Fixed as suggested** (ADR-043): `AssetLog` in `ZTronAssetModel/Environment/AssetLog.swift` with `hashed(_:)`, `emit(_:tap:)` and `log(_:_:_:tap:)`; `LogLine.subsystem`. Test `R-OBS-1: log lines carry the module subsystem and their category and render identifiers as stable hashes` *(exists; logs at `.debug`, which the unified log does not persist by default)*. |
| 11 | C2 in `prepare(kinds:)` and `LiveAppVersionSource`; R-STO-6 scope; `static var live` | **Fixed:** the bundle enumeration runs in one unit on the client's bulk executor (an internal accessor, same module); `LiveAppVersionSource(executor:)` reads `Info.plist` on the metadata executor; ADR-038 records R-STO-6's scope; `.live` became `static func live(legacyDefaultsSuiteName: String? = nil)` and `init`'s default is `.live()`. |
| 12 | ADR-014 option (d) vs the single `#available`; device-only line share | **Fixed:** option (d) rejected in ADR-014, and the R-APL-5 scan is stated to cover all of `Sources/`. Device-only files get a working ceiling of **100 executable lines each** (§14), and the first Stage 2 coverage run must report the `device-only-excluded` share; today both files are stubs (53 and 41 source lines). |

Further amendments made while resolving this review: `EngineIntrospection.memoryPressureObserverActive`, `EngineDiagnostics`, `AssetStore.setRetentionScope(_:)`, `PrefetchHandle` is built through `package init(registry:ids:priority:)`.

### 13.3 Contract-fix round 3

The third independent review of the Stage 1 contracts (`.build-orchestrator/round3-issues.json`) raised 8 issues (1 blocking). All 8 are fixed; nothing is declined. ADRs: ADR-046…048 (new), ADR-010, ADR-016, ADR-026, ADR-032, ADR-035, ADR-043, ADR-045 (amended). Where a fix deviates from the reviewer's suggested shape, the reason is given.

| # | Issue | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Blocking.** Ownership: WP-C's API was 16 stubs inside `Store/AssetStore.swift` (WP-B's directory) over `private` state; `Staging/` and `Layout/` were unowned | **Fixed** (ADR-046, §1.1, §1.2). WP-C's methods moved, signatures and stubs unchanged, into `extension AssetStore` files: `Eviction/StoreEviction.swift` (`evictionPlan`, `setRetentionScope`, `setPinned`, `evictIfNeeded`), `Cleanup/StoreLeases.swift` (`acquireLease`, `releaseLease`), `Cleanup/StoreCleanup.swift` (`collectStaging`, `collectGarbage`, `purge`, `storageReport`), `Integrity/StoreIntegrity.swift` (`reportUnreadable`, `verifyIntegrity`, `recordIntegrityFailure`), `Migration/StoreMigration.swift` (`migrateLegacy`, `adoptLegacyNow`). The state is declared: `AssetStore.environment` and `AssetStore.state: StoreState` are internal; `StoreState` holds `core` (WP-B: phase, layout, lock, epoch, committed generation, degraded, applied manifest, ETag, records, stamp, sync-due, purge epoch, blob table, publishing, metrics) and WP-C's `cleanup` (leases, lease token, cleanup stamps), `eviction` (retention scope), `integrity` (verification cursor), `migration` (persisted phase and stamp, progress), each sub-struct in its owner's directory. WP-B provides `StoreInternals.swift` primitives for WP-C; WP-B calls WP-C only through two opt-in hooks (`runLegacyPhaseOne()`, `applyEviction(_:)`). `Staging/` and `Layout/` are WP-B's. ARCHITECTURE §1 lists every file's owner and the L1…L5 plan; §11's WP column follows. **Deviation:** `recordAccesses` moved to WP-B, not WP-C (its only caller is WP-B's acknowledgement path, R6), and the deletion machinery, streaming hash and launch reconciliation are WP-B's, so R-INT-1/2/5/6, R-CLN-1, R-CLN-7, P-6, P-13 and half of R-CLN-5 moved from C to B (§1.1 rule 3). The reviewer's alternative (sequence C after B and grant it `AssetStore.swift`) was not taken: sequencing is now the plan anyway (ADR-046), but disjoint files keep ownership decidable by path. |
| 2 | Snapshot install rate: no lane input, no interval in the store | **Fixed** (ADR-016 amended, §5). `StoreConfiguration.snapshotInstallInterval` (default 0.1 s) is fed from `configuration.tuning` (clamped; `SyncTuning.clamped()` now also clamps a negative or NaN interval) by `AssetClient.storeConfiguration(for:)`; `publish(_:binding:expecting:urgency: AssetPriority)` installs at once for `.userInitiated` and at most once per interval otherwise; the trailing install is reported by `nextDeadline()` and run by `runDueWork()` (`StoreWorkReport.installedSnapshot`). Chose the urgency input over `installSnapshotNow()`: the lane is known at the call and a second call would add an await to every user publish. Tests: `R-STO-13: the store receives the client's snapshot install interval, manifest limits, policy and legacy layout` and `R-SYN-11: the eviction switch defaults to permitted and an out-of-range snapshot install interval is clamped` *(exist)*; regression `R-STO-13: bulk publishes install at most one snapshot per snapshotInstallInterval and a user-initiated publish installs at once` (WP-B, §11). |
| 3 | Persisted permanent-missing: no store operation records a 404 | **Fixed as suggested, general form** (ADR-032 amended, §7.6): `AssetStore.recordFailure(_ ids:identity:reason:) async throws(StoreError)` (WP-C, `Integrity/`) marks records still at `identity` `failed(reason)` and persists it; `apply` (WP-B) clears `failed` and `quarantined` when the content identity changes. `reason` is `.notFound` (R-NET-6) or `.unreadable`; integrity keeps its backing-off `recordIntegrityFailure`. Also clarified: the engine, not `publish`, records integrity bookkeeping, so WP-B never calls a WP-C stub. Tests (§11): `R-NET-6: recordFailure persists notFound across a reopen and a changed entry clears it` (C) and `R-NET-6: a 404 is not requested again until its manifest entry changes, across a relaunch` (F). |
| 4 | Five configuration knobs not wired | **Fixed** (ADR-048, NEEDS-HUMAN-REVIEW for the public signatures). (a) `AssetClient.init(configuration:environment: AssetEnvironment? = nil)`; `nil` resolves to `.live(legacyDefaultsSuiteName: configuration.legacy.defaultsSuiteName)` (`resolvedEnvironment(_:for:)`); the field is kept, not removed, because apps with a suite need it. (b) `PrefetchWindowController(client:radius: Int? = nil)` defaults to `configuration.prefetchRadius` (`resolvedRadius(_:configuration:)`). (c) `StoragePolicy.evictionPermitted` (default `true`) and `PlanningPolicy(storage:includeOnDemand:now:)`. (d) `StoreConfiguration.limits: ManifestLimits` (the publication cap `maxAssetBytes`, and the limits legacy migration decodes under), fed by `storeConfiguration(for:)`. (e) `StoreLayout.indexSchemaVersion: UInt16 = 1`. Tests *(exist)*: `R-STO-11: the default live environment reads the legacy stamp from the configured UserDefaults suite`, `R-CAR-3: the window controller's radius defaults to the client configuration's prefetchRadius`, `R-SYN-11: the planning policy takes eviction permission, unverified admission and the prefix rule from StoragePolicy`, `R-STO-13: the store receives …` (which also checks the exported schema version). |
| 5 | Background session wiring | **Fixed** (ADR-026 amended, §6.1, §6.4). WP-D owns every Transport body, Stage 1 ones included. `SessionDelegate` gained the session-event seam created in `init` (`package let sessionEvents: AsyncStream<SessionEvent>`, internal `sessionEventSink`); `ForegroundTransferBackend.invalidate()` awaits its `.invalidated`, `LiveBackgroundSessionDriver.activate()` returns it. The convenience init builds the coordinator over `LiveBackgroundSessionDriver` when `.enabled` (`makeBackgroundBackend(_:staging:policy:)`, implemented: construction only, no `URLSession`); `BackgroundTransferCoordinator.init` gained `options:` (task cap, redirect exposure). The coordinator's and driver's bodies stay WP-D stubs. Test: `R-NET-3: the production source builds a background coordinator only when background transfers are enabled with a valid namespace` *(exists)*. |
| 6 | Test-support seams and benchmark structure | **Fixed** (ADR-045, ADR-010 amended). (a) `FakeRemoteSource.scriptReattached(_:)` (WP-H stub, documented: returned once per launch). (b) `FakeTransferStep.stageGenerated(seed:byteCount:)` (WP-H) streams `GeneratedPayload` bytes in 256 KiB chunks; `GeneratedPayload.bytes(seed:offset:length:)` and `sha256(seed:byteCount:)` are implemented and tested (`Test support: generated payloads are a pure function of seed and offset and hash in bounded chunks like one-shot SHA-256`). (c) `@Suite(.serialized) struct ZTronAssetKitBenchmarks {}` in `Tests/ZTronAssetKitBenchmarks/ZTronAssetKitBenchmarks.swift`; `BudgetsBenchmarks` is nested under it; per-WP files and budget assignment are in §1.2 and §11. Verified: `--filter Benchmarks` runs `Suite ZTronAssetKitBenchmarks` → `Suite "BudgetsBenchmarks"` (1 test in 2 suites), and `--skip Benchmarks` excludes it. |
| 7 | Five TestSupport awaits without `// REENTRANCY:` | **Fixed:** comments on all five (rows TS2, TS3) plus the two `onTermination` tasks (TS1), and §10.3 is the TestSupport ledger. |
| 8 | Tests would write outside the repository (unified log; background download temp files) | **Fixed** (ADR-047, §2, §14). `LogSink` (`write`, `signposter`), live `OSLogSink`, test `TapOnlyLogSink` (the default of `AssetEnvironment.init`), `AssetEnvironment.logSink`/`logRoute`, `LogRoute`; `AssetLog.emit(_:sink:tap:)` replaces the always-os.Logger `emit(_:tap:)`; the route reaches the store (`StoreEnvironment.log`), `RemoteSourceFactory.makeRemoteSource(configuration:staging:log:)`, `HTTPRemoteSource.log`, `AppleHostedRemoteSource.log`, `AppleHostedTier.resolve(…, log:, …)`. The existing R-OBS-1 test now uses `TapOnlyLogSink`, so the suite emits nothing to the unified log; new test `R-OBS-1: every line reaches the environment's sink and tap once, and an environment of fakes keeps the unified log untouched` *(exists)*. §14 states that background-delegate tests call the delegate methods directly with unresumed tasks and files inside this repository, never real download tasks. |

Further amendments made while resolving this review: `StoreWorkReport.installedSnapshot`, `StoreEnvironment.log` replaces `logTap`, `BackgroundTransferCoordinator.driver`/`options` are readable (`package`), `FailureReason` and `RetryDisposition.untilEntryChanges` document the persisted 404.

### 13.4 Contract-fix round 4

The fourth independent review of the Stage 1 contracts raised 7 issues (1 blocking). All 7 are fixed; nothing is declined. No new ADR: ADR-012, ADR-033, ADR-045 and ADR-046 carry round-4 amendments, and ADR-047 is tagged NEEDS-HUMAN-REVIEW. No production public or package signature changed; the TestSupport additions are listed under issue 6.

| # | Issue | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Blocking.** Ownership: MUST tests whose owner has no test path (R-CAR-9 and R-SEC-3 were WP-I's, R-ENV-4 "all"'s; R-ENV-2/3/5 unwritten) | **Fixed, first option** (ADR-046 amendment, §1.2, §11). The orchestrator wrote all six package-wide scans in `Tests/ZTronAssetModelTests/PackageScanContractTests.swift`, which is orchestrator-owned like every `*ContractTests.swift`: `R-CAR-9: no production source imports UIKit, SwiftUI or Combine`, `R-SEC-3: no file under Sources/ declares NSAllowsArbitraryLoads, an ATS exception or a private API`, `R-ENV-2: …deployment floors are iOS 15 and macOS 12…`, `R-ENV-3: Package.swift declares no package dependencies`, `R-ENV-4: the unsafe-pattern scan of Sources/ finds at most three escapes, each audited…` (patterns and cap read from `Scripts/quality-gates.json`, so it cannot drift from Q-3) and `R-ENV-5: Package.swift sets Swift 6 language mode and strict concurrency and no warnings-as-errors flag…`. All six pass. None is vacuous: each scan first requires a file of every one of the 7 source targets, and a temporary probe file (`import Combine`, an unmarked `@unchecked Sendable`, an `NSAllowsArbitraryLoads` literal) made R-CAR-9, R-ENV-4 and R-SEC-3 fail with one issue each before it was deleted. §11 now gives the six to the orchestrator and §1.2's row for I owns no test. TRACE now lists 46 MUSTs without a test (was 52); each of the 46 and each of the 14 budgets has a writer with a test path at a level where its dependencies are merged (issue 4). |
| 2 | Ownership rules of the B/C split omit writes WP-C needs (legacy phase 1/3, stamps) | **Fixed as suggested** (§1.1 rules 1 and 2, §3, §4.7; ADR-046 and ADR-012 amendments). Rule 1 and the docs of `StoreCoreState`, `StoreState`, `AssetStore` and `runLegacyPhaseOne()`/`migrateLegacy(maxFiles:)` now grant legacy phase 1 the writes it has no other route for. It installs the legacy manifest as `appliedManifest` (`etag` `nil`), but only while none is applied, so newer state is never overridden. It inserts records only for ids that manifest lists and the index lacks, always as `absent(.neverFetched)` with `expectedBlob`. It sets `degraded` on an undecodable legacy manifest. Phase 3 may set `syncDue` directly or call `markSyncDue()`. `lastCleanupAt` is stamped only by WP-C's GC, eviction and purge, matching `StorageReport.lastCleanupAt` ("the last GC, eviction or purge"); "prune reap" was dropped from its doc, so WP-B never writes a WP-C field. `lastMaintenanceAt` was **deleted**, not given a `recordMaintenance(finishedAt:)` API: nothing reads it and no requirement needs it. STAMPS drops the field (ADR-012 amendment; the schema is not shipped). |
| 3 | C3 REENTRANCY ledger gaps; no route for a WP to add rows | **Fixed** (§10, §1.2). Pre-added store rows **R16** (`verifyIntegrity` hash units: same blob, still `live`, not degraded), **R17** (WP-C's trailing `requireCommitted`/`publishSnapshot` awaits) and **R18** (`adoptLegacyNow`'s units). Pre-added engine rows **RE-S35** (`recordFailure`, `recordIntegrityFailure`), **RE-S36** (`location`, `adoptLegacyNow`), **RE-S37** (`planningView`, `evictionPlan`, `admit`), **RE-S38** (read-only entry points: `availability`, `planningView`, `metrics`), **RE-S39** (`collectStaging` after launch reconciliation, `markSyncDue`), **RE-S40** (`nextDeadline` re-arming outside the alarm), **RE-S41** (`remote.cancelTransfer`) and **RE-S42** (`remote.handleBackgroundEvents`). For awaits nobody can foresee, a WP proposes the row in its `docs/adr-drafts/<wp>.md` and cites it as `<wp>-n (proposed)`. The orchestrator numbers it into §10 at merge. An await covered by a proposed row is not a review failure. |
| 4 | Ownership table inconsistencies (§11 vs §1.2/ADRs) | **Fixed, all five, and generalised** (§11; ADR-033 and ADR-046 amendments). (a) R-CAR-6 → K; ADR-033 now names WP-K's adapters. (b) P-7 → F, target Sync, in `BenchSync.swift`. (c) The R-STO-1 foreground-transfer test → F (L4). (d) H ships the conformance suite as `FileSystemConformance.violations(of:in:)` in `ZTronAssetTestSupport` (WP-H stub, returns values so TestSupport stays free of Swift Testing, ADR-041). H runs it on `FakeFileSystem` and B on `LiveFileSystem` from `Core/`. (e) The R-CLN-10 retention-scope test → F. It asserts the `AdoptionReport` handed to `reclaimSourceCopies` (RE-S34); the planner's handling of exclusions is already tested by E. **Generalised:** every multi-WP row of §11 now names each test's writer, so no other test has the same problem. This moved the R-CLN-8 re-download, the R-CLN-9 source-copy and both R-CAR-5 tests to F, and R-STO-7, R-CAR-7/8, R-OBS-2/3, R-SEC-1 (canary), R-CLN-11 and R-SYN-15's API walk to G. R-OBS-1's log-call-site scan and R-ENV-1's scan are A's. |
| 5 | Merge conflicts on generated/shared docs paths across worktrees | **Fixed as suggested** (§1.2). `docs/TRACEABILITY.md` is orchestrator-owned and regenerated after each merge. A WP that ran `--gates-only` or `--full` restores it before handoff (`git checkout -- docs/TRACEABILITY.md`), or runs only `--quick`. `docs/adr-drafts/**` is carved out of WP-I's glob, one file per WP, owned by that WP. |
| 6 | Testability seams to add | **Fixed.** (a) Implemented now rather than deferred to WP-H, because the shared lock has to reach `FakeTransferHandle`, which is complete. `FakeTransportLog` is one `Mutex` over `[FakeTransportEvent]` (`.started(TransferRequest)`, `.controlled(FakeControlRecord)`). `FakeRemoteSource.transportLog` shares it with every handle the source creates (`FakeTransferHandle(id:attempt:transportLog:)`, defaulted). `starts` and `controlLog` are now projections of it. Test: `Test support: a fake source and its handles record starts and controls in one global order`. `transfer(_:)`'s body, which records the start and passes the log, stays WP-H's (ADR-045 amendment). (b) Documented (§6.5, §11 R-NET-9). The R-NET-9 test posts `ProcessInfo.powerStateDidChangeNotification` or `thermalStateDidChangeNotification` on the source's injected `center`, never `.default`. WP-D may add a `package` path-ingest seam in `Conditions/`. |
| 7 | Docs coherence: ADR-047 public API not tagged for review | **Fixed.** ADR-047 is tagged **NEEDS-HUMAN-REVIEW (public signatures)** with a note on the signatures and the silent-by-default hand-built environment. `DECISIONS.md` now opens with an **owner review list** of every tagged ADR, ADR-047 included. |

Verified in this round (scratch `.build-cfix4`, `TMPDIR` and the Clang module cache inside it): `swift build` debug and `-c release` with `-Xswiftc -warnings-as-errors` → `Build complete!`; `xcodebuild -scheme ZTronAssetKit -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' -derivedDataPath .build-cfix4/xcode build OTHER_SWIFT_FLAGS='-warnings-as-errors'` → exit 0, the six production modules built for `arm64-apple-ios`; `SWIFTPM_SCRATCH=.build-cfix4 bash Scripts/verify.sh --quick` → `VERIFY: PASS` (62 tests in 8 suites, benchmarks skipped); `bash Scripts/verify.sh --gates-only` → selftest, harness, unsafe (`escapes found=0 max=3`) and docs (`1227/1227`) pass, and only TRACE fails, on the 46 MUSTs and 14 budgets that Stage 2 work packages write.

### 13.5 Orchestrator follow-ups to contract review round 5

Round 5 found 0 blocking issues and 6 non-blocking ones; the orchestrator applied all six before Stage 2.

| # | Issue | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | WP-F's storage-root tests need the real foreground backend | `ZTronAssetTransport` added to the **test** target `ZTronAssetSyncTests` only; Sync's production dependencies are unchanged. At the level-4 merge `ZTronAssetAppleHosted` joined it as a second test-only dependency, for the end-to-end R-APL-3 test (L4-R1, orchestrator commit `7cf647c`; ADR-053); `ZTronAssetTestSupport` gains no dependency. |
| 2 | R-OBS-1 signpost intervals unobservable in tests | WP-A adds an interval seam in the Model logging route (e.g. `LogRoute.interval(_:category:)` beginning/ending on the sink's signposter **and** recording interval-began/ended events to the `LogTap`), and a test that each of the six interval names (plan, transfer, verify, commit, gc, eviction) has a call site; every WP that owns one of those operations wraps it in the seam and asserts it through the tap. This is an addition to a contract file, authorised here. |
| 3 | Mandatory scenarios without a feasible writer | Explicit writers (in addition to §11): **C** — `R-STO-5: crash at every boundary during eviction preserves I-1…I-5`, `R-STO-5: crash at every boundary during legacy migration preserves I-1…I-5 and adopts every verified file`, and the model-based oracle including evict (`R-STO-5: seeded random store, relocate, remove, evict, crash and reopen sequences match the oracle with I-1…I-5 after every step`), under `Tests/ZTronAssetStorageTests/Cleanup/`. **F** — `R-NET-3: a live background task is re-attached after relaunch, not duplicated`, `R-NET-3: an offline launch cancels no live background task`, and the end-to-end `R-NET-3: a download completed while the app was dead is adopted from staging, not re-downloaded` (with `FakeRemoteSource.scriptReattached`); **D** keeps only the transport-level half (`R-NET-3: the coordinator reports a download finished while the app was dead as a reattached staged transfer`). **B** — `R-CLN-10: a publish that lands during the adoption report's durable commit is not reported adopted`, `R-CLN-5: two identifiers with identical bytes published concurrently keep the blob until both are removed`, `R-INT-2: a manifest change during the publish rename yields superseded and never returns the URL`. |
| 4 | `adoptVerifiedFile` vs snapshot urgency | It publishes no snapshot; callers call `publishSnapshot(urgency:)` (doc in `StoreInternals.swift` and §4.3). |
| 5 | Background `activate()` is not on `RemoteSource` | §6.4 and RE-S28 reworded: `reattachedTransfers()` activates the session. |
| 6 | Shared memory probe in the benchmarks module | **B** writes `Tests/ZTronAssetKitBenchmarks/MemoryProbe.swift` (the `task_info` resident-size probe, internal API) at L2 together with `BenchStorage.swift`; **F** and **G** reuse it read-only. No other file in the benchmarks module may declare a memory helper. |

## 14. Coverage plan and seams (Q-2)

- **Excluded (device-only):** `Sources/ZTronAssetTransport/BackgroundSessionBackend.swift` and `Sources/ZTronAssetAppleHosted/LiveAssetPackBackend.swift`, each a thin, decision-free conformer (SPEC ceiling ≤ 250 lines; 53 and 41 source lines at Stage 1; after the level-2 merge 127 and 97 physical lines; the drafts report 96 executable lines for the first (WP-D's coverage export) and 81 llvm-cov lines for the second, both under the 100-line working ceiling, while WP-E's last close-out run printed `COVERAGE device-only-excluded lines=110 share=1.69% max=3.0% OK` for the two together; as those figures come from different exports, the orchestrator quotes the merged head's own `COVERAGE device-only-excluded … share=` line; ADR-050). `BackgroundSessionBackend.swift` makes no decision: tags come from `BackgroundTaskTag.forStart`/`label(_:priority:)` and the configuration from `BackgroundSessionConfiguration.make(identifier:discretionary:)`, all on the tested side. `ZTronAssetTestSupport` is excluded. **Working ceiling: 100 executable lines per file** (contract-fix round 2, issue 12). Both files compile on the macOS host (BackgroundAssets is importable on macOS 27), so llvm-cov counts their lines against `device_only_max_lines_fraction` (3 %): two files at the 250-line SPEC ceiling would need about 16,000 production lines to pass, while 200 executable lines need about 6,500. Measured in round 2 over the stub contracts (the gate's own export over all 8 test bundles, into `.build-probe-cfix2/`): `COVERAGE device-only-excluded lines=50 share=1.56% max=3.0% OK`, against 3,162 counted production lines. The first Stage 2 coverage run reports the share again (`COVERAGE device-only-excluded … share=`), and a WP whose file grows past 100 executable lines moves logic into the tested side (`BackgroundTransferCoordinator`, `AppleHostedRemoteSource`).
- **Every other production line runs on macOS, and on the iOS Simulator, with fakes** (ADR-051: no test is excluded, skipped or conditioned on the platform; the `#filePath` source scans, `Tests/Fixtures` and the live store directories under `.build-tests/storage-core/` work unchanged in a simulator process). **Test rules for every WP:** ADR-051's gating convention (`docs/adr-drafts/ios.md` item 2: both platforms in every `@available`, on a `@Test` function or the helper that uses the fake, never on a `@Suite`; CPU-bound case loops yield once per case) and its simulator run before hand-off (§1.2). Storage: `FakeFileSystem` + `InlineBlockingExecutor` + `ManualTimeSource`; `LiveFileSystem` against per-test directories **inside this repository** (`.build-tests/` and the other git-ignored `.build-*` fixture directories, §12.6 S2-26; ADR-050), never the system temp directory; the errno and Cocoa-error mappings are pure functions. Sync: `FakeRemoteSource` (attached to the real store's staging sink by `FakeRemoteSourceFactory`, ADR-045), `FakeDeviceConditionsSource` and `FakeMemoryPressureSource` (census waits), a real `AssetStore` over `FakeFileSystem`, `SteppedBlockingExecutor` to hold store units, `ScriptedSnapshotSink` where the facade's mirror is out of reach; one post-await revalidation funnel, an injectable planner (`SyncPlanning`), illegal transitions counted (sync-2 S2-26). **Sync test rules since the level-4 merge (ADR-053; L4-R17, `f.md` F-31…F-34, F-41):** (1) the clock drivers (`EngineHarness.drive`/`withClock`, the benchmarks' `SyncBench.drive`) never wake an in-flight manifest fetch's deadline or a probe's watchdog (the clock fence, a test-target extension); they decide the predicate, the fence, the sleeper count and the advance in one engine turn, and they bound clock moves, not waits (F-31, F-32); (2) WP-F's tests wait on conditions (`awaitCondition`, censuses, gates, held seams, handles), each ending on cancellation, never on yields, rounds or what a consumer happened to pull (L4-R17); (3) a test waiting for a live transfer's progress ends its stalled prefix on the foreground transport's progress quantum (`ProgressQuantum`: the first chunk and each boundary of `max(256 KiB, expected / 64)` are reported, nothing between; 256 KiB at the sizes the tests use) (F-32); (4) the engine harnesses spawn the engine's owned tasks at the default priority or above (`EngineState.ownedTaskPriorityFloor`, a seam that is `nil` in production), while the benchmarks keep production priorities (F-33); (5) the invariant check leaves to the alarm's next turn a pending run whose throttle or breaker gate the clock opened (F-33, F-34); (6) a clock move to an instant lands on it exactly: `ManualTimeSource.advance(to:)` goes through a `Double` and can stop 1 ns short (WP-H's file; Stage 3, L4-R24(2)), so WP-F's tests use exact moves (F-41). **The gate model (L4-R20; F-40…F-42; §11 R-SYN-6):** `R-SYN-6: seeded walks over clock moves, held alarm turns, probe outcomes, 429s and space events never settle, start or park against a gate that admits at now, and never repeat runs without the clock moving` (160 seeded walks; a failure prints its seed; `GATE_MODEL_SEEDS` replays chosen walks) and its one-reserved-slot variant `R-SYN-6: the seeded gate walks with one slot reserved for user-initiated work, so jobs wait in the queue for a slot without being parked, keep every invariant` check after every step: I1 no run settles blocked by a gate that admits at `now`; I2 no pending run starts while its gate is closed at `now`; I3 no parked claim stays parked once its gate admits and the alarm has had its turn; I4 at most one manifest request per step and at most two runs while the clock does not move (F-41); I5 with no run in place, no pending run waits on an origin gate that admits at `now` (F-41); I6 in the turn a run starts, a breaker admitting only a probe already holds it for the run's manifest fetch (F-42). Storage: the same executor and sink for the REENTRANCY rows R2–R15. Transport: the loopback server (data tasks), `FakeBackgroundSessionDriver`, pure classifier/parser/guard tables, `LiveDeviceConditionsSource` on the host. **The `.background` delegate paths are tested by calling `SessionDelegate`'s methods directly** — with an *unresumed* task made by an ephemeral session (`downloadTask(with:)` without `resume()`, so no transfer and no CFNetwork temp file) and a location file the test created inside this repository (`.build-tests/` and the other git-ignored `.build-*` fixture directories, §12.6 S2-26; ADR-050) — and reading `sessionEvents`. **Never with real download tasks**: an interrupted one leaves `CFNetworkDownload_*.tmp` in the per-user temp directory, outside the repository (transport-2 T2-01, owner directive). AppleHosted: `FakePackBackend`. Kit: a real client over fakes; `PrefetchRegistry` fakes for handle ordering; the cache relay directly. **Kit test rules since the level-5 merge (ADR-054; L5-R14, `docs/adr-drafts/g.md` G-9, G-16 and its L5-R14 table):** `KitHarness` builds a real client over `FakeFileSystem`, `ManualTimeSource` and `FakeRemoteSource`, and never moves the clock at or past the engine's clock fence (L4-R17); every wait is a condition wait that ends on cancellation, never yields, rounds or what a consumer happened to pull; shared fake logs (`starts`, `controls`, `transportLog`) are counted from a baseline taken at a defined point; a root replacement is one rename of the fake volume (`vanishRoot()`); interleavings are placed with the `LifecycleSeamPoint` seam (`nil` in production; six points, G-10). **The event model (L5-R13, L5-R19; §11 R-SYN-12):** `R-SYN-12: seeded walks over subscriptions, pulls, drops, cancellations, parked reopens, publishing calls and shutdown lose no event a stream is owed, keep each stream's generations in order, leak no follower or subscriber, and buffer at most one event per forwarder` (500 seeds in ten cases of 50, each under a 2-minute limit, and 5,000 by hand; a failure prints its seed; `EVENT_MODEL_SEEDS` replays chosen walks, `EVENT_MODEL_SEED_COUNT` runs more, `EVENT_MODEL_TRACE` appends each walk to a file) checks I1 no loss, I2 order, I3 no leak and I4 at most one event in each forwarder, with the tolerances G-17's audit lists and the two measurement limits ADR-054 records (L5-R22 (2)). **Compatibility test rules since the level-5 WP-K merge (ADR-055; L5K-R2, L5K-R3; `docs/adr-drafts/k.md` K-10 and its L5K-R2 table):** `CompatHarness` builds a real `AssetClient` over `FakeFileSystem`, `FakeRemoteSource`, `FakeDeviceConditionsSource`, `InlineBlockingExecutor` (or `SteppedBlockingExecutor` as the store's bulk executor, to hold legacy migration at a unit), `SeededRandom` and a `ManualTimeSource` wrapped in `AutoAdvancingClock`, which moves the clock only inside `sleep(until:)`, to the deadline of the sleep being registered when it lies within 1 s, and never reads the wall clock or sleeps (C4); the target uses the public API only (no `@testable`, and no `KitHarness`, whose fenced driver needs `@testable` engine state; K-1). The harness's `manifestFetchDeadline` of 10^6 s is a setting no test of a deadline relies on; `snapshotInstallInterval` is 0 and `maxAttemptsPerRun` 1, with one test at the production install interval (PK-R14b: the interval of 0 hid L5K-R16's class). Every assertion waits on an explicit, publicly observable condition (a call's return, the coordinator's published progress, an `EventProbe` event, `availability`, `lookup`, a census, the diagnostic report), never on an advance having happened; L5-R14's patterns (a)–(g) were checked for every test and helper ((d) and (g) occur nowhere); any flake under load or TSan blocks (L5K-R3).
- **Logging in tests (ADR-047):** every test environment uses `TapOnlyLogSink` (the default of `AssetEnvironment.init`; `LogRoute.tapOnly(_:)` for sources built directly), so no test writes to the unified log; R-OBS-1 and R-SEC-1 assert on the `LogTap`. `OSLogSink` is exercised only by `.live()` code paths that never run in the deterministic suite.
- **Known deliberately untested lines:** `LiveLegacyDefaults` (`Sources/ZTronAssetStorage/Migration/LiveLegacyDefaults.swift`, 11 of its 14 llvm-cov lines; the coverage export counts 3 of 14 covered, `docs/adr-drafts/c.md` cycle 8): exercising it would write `~/Library/Preferences`, outside this repository (owner directive; ADR-052). `LiveFileSystem.standardDirectory` (7 lines, `Sources/ZTronAssetStorage/FileSystem/LiveFileSystem.swift:23-29`): exercising it would create directories under `~/Library`, outside this repository (owner directive; ADR-050). Also from the level-2 merge (ADR-050; D-1, E-13): `SessionDelegate.redirectDecision`'s guard for a task without an original or current URL (`Sources/ZTronAssetTransport/Session/SessionDelegate+DataTasks.swift:207-211`; URLSession always has both when it asks about a redirect); the `@unknown default` of `ConditionsMapping.thermal`, which maps an unknown future state to `.critical` (`Conditions/LiveDeviceConditionsSource.swift:22-23`); the pre-cancelled branches of the test seams `AppleSourceState.waitForDrain` (`Sources/ZTronAssetAppleHosted/AppleSourceState.swift:123-124`) and `PackAvailabilityCoordinator.wait(for:registered:)` (`PackAvailabilityCoordinator.swift:130-131`), guards against a cancellation hop that runs before registration, which no test reaches deterministically; the implicit closure of `packBackend ?? LiveAssetPackBackend()` in `AppleHostedTier.resolve` (`AppleHostedTier.swift:46`), which builds the device-only live backend that no test constructs (APPLE-API-VERIFICATION §1.7(b)); and `AppleSourceState.endStaging`'s early return while another staging call still runs (`AppleSourceState.swift:70-72`), reached only when two copies overlap by scheduling, so a coverage run counts 1245 or 1246 of the tier's 1249 lines. Added at the level-4 merge (ADR-053; `docs/adr-drafts/f.md` F-14, F-27…F-30; the Sync per-target gate passes at 93.22–93.35 %, and per-file margins are not a gate, L4-R12): the stale-entry branches of the re-admission turn, an owed job that left its state before its follow-up (`Sources/ZTronAssetSync/Engine/EngineReadmission.swift:128-129`, `:140-142`); `formUnion` when a breaker or throttle event arrives while re-queues are still owed (`:188`); the Apple tier's own lane check refusing a lane in `laneAdmitsAfterConditions` (`:199-201`); a re-admission follow-up that cannot be spawned (shutting down) or finds the engine stopped (`:216-219`); the invariant check's message for a waiter interest without a waiter (`EngineCommands.swift:1624` since the level-5 WP-K merge moved it from `:1602`; ADR-055) and its filter's branch for a stale set-aside entry; `joinGroup`'s branch for a manifest copy that changed between two batches (`EngineCommands.swift:287-291`; count 0 at the level-4 merge, run once in the level-5 WP-K merged head's coverage run, so schedule-dependent; ADR-055); the space settlement's `continue` when a wake was armed during its read (`EngineJobs.swift:654-657`; run once in the level-5 WP-K merged head's coverage run, so schedule-dependent; ADR-055). **Defence in depth without a pinning test** (no reachable difference; F-39, F-40, F-41): FULFIL (the `.breaker` turn and `pendingRunDeadlineFired` start through the gate reading), JOIN (a joiner of a circuit-parked job), USER (the backoff skip), CLEAR (the promotion reading), OBC (`openBreakerCause` with its gate check) and MWD (the manifest probe's watchdog, masked because `park(.circuitOpen)` schedules the deadline); the F-39 re-check in `pendingRunDeadlineFired` was not added, because it would be an unreachable branch (G4). Added at the level-5 merge (ADR-054; `docs/adr-drafts/g.md`'s untested-lines paragraph, refreshed for L5-R9, L5-R15 and L5-R21, with L5-R22 (1); the Kit gate passes at 97.83 %, 1,444 of 1,476 lines, one sample of a schedule-dependent range): **not run, though counted covered:** `Sources/ZTronAssetKit/Facade/AssetClient.swift:146-151`, the fall-through after `reopenAttempts` (no test exhausts it; llvm-cov prints an underflowed counter, 18.4E, for `:146`, `:147`, `:150` and `:151`, so the gate counts those four lines covered, about 0.27 % of the target), and `:148-149`, the refusal after every reopen allowed (a root that vanishes again during each reopen), read 0; **schedule-dependent:** `AssetClient.swift:142` (`perform`'s retired-engine repetition) and `ClientLifecycle.swift:409` and `:417` (`setRetentionScope` meeting a shutdown at its entry or after its wait, whichever `R-SYN-15: a shutdown during a reopen …` reaches; the outcome it asserts is the same); **interleavings no test reaches deterministically** (L5-R9: no seam point without a bug proven there): `AssetClient.swift:119` (the reopen-first path of `withAsset` finding the client shut down), `:396` (the report's `.shutDown` from metrics or storage after diagnostics answered), `ClientLifecycle.swift:177` (`reopen` after shutdown), `:185` (a caller whose pair was already replaced), `:216` (a shutdown during the reopen's mirror reset), `:344-345` (`register(_:)`'s `.shutDown` from an engine no reopen retired, that is the client's shutdown between the follow turn and the answer, and its exit for any other failure), `:425` (the engine answering `setRetentionScope` with `.shutDown`), `:508-509` and `:512` (`bind`'s loop-top shutdown and released-group returns, reached only after a `continue`), `:515` (`bind`'s early return for a group already bound to the current engine: unreachable while a reopen's loop stops at a newer generation, kept as an invariant guard, L5-R9) and `:543` (its `continue` for a registration refused by an engine a reopen retired); **invariant guards:** `ClientLifecycle.swift:246-248` (the hand-over meeting a follower whose forwarder is gone without giving its entry back; it guards the weak registry), `:253` (the replacement's `subscribe()` refusing during the hand-over: a `created` engine refuses only once shut down) and `EventForwarder.swift:240` (a handed stream not newer than the one the forwarder holds: a hand-over always carries a newer generation, G-16); **others:** `Observability/DiagnosticReportBuilder.swift:100` (the unparseable-URL fallback), `Fallback/FallbackChain.swift:63-64` (`MainBundleLookup`'s loop body: the test process's main bundle lists no resource of the requested extensions; Stage 1 code) and the closing braces after two `while true` loops (`ClientLifecycle.swift:432`, `:549`), which llvm-cov counts; **run but not pinned** (L5-R22 (1)): `EventForwarder.swift:159-165`, `next()`'s exit for a consumer that stopped during its `events` registration, runs in every schedule since the `stopped` seam, but no assertion depends on it (mutant M05 survives; Stage 3). In the engine (L5-R15, L5-R21): the merge of a join's large give-up leftovers into a later large release of the same group (`Sources/ZTronAssetSync/Engine/EngineCommands.swift:355-356`) has no test, because it needs a join that joined more than `releasesPerTurn` members before it gave up, which no running engine reaches (the line runs in every large release); the release-gate guard (`EngineDispatch.swift:274`) is pinned through the split seam, which pins the rule, not a reachable loss. **Test gap (L5-R22 (3)):** the facade's file-system trigger list for `sync` is pinned only for `.storageUnavailable` (mutant M01s survives; Stage 3). Added at the level-5 WP-K merge (ADR-055; `docs/adr-drafts/k.md` L5K-R19, the L5K-R16 table and the cycle-3 to cycle-5 evidence; the merged head's gate run: Sync 93.42 %, 8,612 of 9,219 lines, Storage 97.36 %, 8,116 of 8,336, Kit 97.83 %, overall 96.51 %, `COVERAGE device-only-excluded lines=177 share=0.68%`; per-file margins are not a gate): **unreachable by construction** (L5K-R19, L5K-C2-07): four defensive branches of L5K-R13 in `Sources/ZTronAssetStorage/Migration/LegacyPhases.swift`, the `else { continuation.resume() }` of `awaitLegacyAdoption` (`:283-284`) and of `awaitLegacyFlight` (`:703-704`) (each closure runs in the actor's isolation right after the caller's check of the same mark, with no suspension between; both callers of `awaitLegacyFlight`, `performAdoptLegacyNow` and L5K-R17's wait, check the mark first) and the no-mark guards of `endLegacyAdoption` (`:294`) and `endLegacyOnDemand` (`:712`) (every caller sets the mark before it can end it); the tripwire's violation message (`EngineCommands.swift:1600`, reached only when the invariant breaks); the pre-transfer read's catches, the answer site's (`EnginePresence.swift:262-265`: an adoption that throws there, the store closing) and `storedLocation`'s (`:331-333`); an id the reads answered present stops being present only through a store mutation that installs the whole state itself, so the loop never reaches a second iteration: entered 108 times in the merged head's run, the first iteration answering every time);
