The shims, outside the gate (a test target, ADR-033), measured in the compatibility bundle (llvm-cov over `Shims/*.swift`: 538 of 548 lines, 98.18 %; L5K-R12 accepts the uncovered lines as listed): not run are the `installed` setter's call on the process point (`CDNAssetResolver.swift:59-61`; its body is the tested seam `setInstalled(_:on:)`, K-2), the deprecated coordinator initialiser's body (`CDNSyncCoordinator.swift:46-49`; it constructs `UserDefaults`, K-9), the default argument of `CDNLocalStore.init(baseDirectory:computeChecksum:)` (`CDNLocalStore.swift:64`, reached only through a protocol), the fallback of `defaultLegacyRoot()` (`CDNLocalStore.swift:150`) and one region of the Stage 1 `CDNAsset.swift` (`:47`, the `?? self.identifier` fallback).
- **Single-catch checks (ADR-050; D-20.2):** two checks are caught by one test each, so neither test may be dropped as redundant. The `byteSize` −1 and `Int64.min` rows of `SourceTests.routeCases` are the only guard on `routesToBackground`'s own size check (mutant M4d: without it `negativeSizeStaysInForeground` still passes, because `startIfAdmitted` refuses the job and the source falls back to the foreground). The checksummed-content half of the background continuation gate (`continuesResumeData` in `SessionDelegate+Downloads.swift`; mutant M1b) is caught only by `continuationNeedsFlagAndChecksummedContent` (`SessionDelegateDirectTests.swift`), because the coordinator never keeps resume data for a checksum-less identity, so no end-to-end test can produce such a tag.
- **Budgets per target:** Storage and Sync ≥ 90 %, every other production target ≥ 70 %, overall ≥ 80 % (`Scripts/quality-gates.json`). Not measured in this round (contracts are stubs); the first coverage report comes from Stage 2.
- **Not verified here (goes to `VERIFICATION.md` / `DEVICE-TEST-PLAN.md`):** any iOS 15 *runtime* behaviour (compile-time only), background-session relaunch re-attachment, Apple-hosted packs end to end, `taskDescription` persistence across relaunch, `F_BARRIERFSYNC`/`F_FULLFSYNC` semantics and costs on device, file protection on device, real network transitions, HTTP/2 against CloudFront. Added at the level-2 merge (ADR-050): background resume data on a device (a 206 with `Content-Range` and the whole body moved; the errors for stale data), `taskDescription` persistence of a task created by `downloadTask(withResumeData:)`, whether `nsurlsessiond` ever finishes a fresh background download with a ranged 206 (a device check before background transfers ship, D-19.1), the live driver's ordering of task creation against `invalidate()` and its `forStart` argument and overload choice (D-20.1), a notification arriving between `NWPathMonitor.start` and its first update (D-18.5), `LiveAssetPackBackend` against real Apple-hosted packs (APPLE-API-VERIFICATION §5), and the `LiveFileSystem` catch paths APFS inside the repository cannot provoke (a write failing after a successful open, the `rmdir` ENOENT race, `lseek`/identity failures after the lock was taken, the `fsync` fallback). Added by the iOS Simulator test build (ADR-051): the tests run on the iOS 27.0 Simulator only (test bundles build at iOS 17.0, so no test runs below iOS 17; the Mutex-gated tests need iOS 18 and the Apple-tier tests iOS 26; what they do on an iOS 17 simulator was not checked); the benchmarks bundle is built, not run, on the simulator; the loopback server's refusal of a LAN address is not probed (only `::1`, which assumes `lo0` carries `::1`); whether an `xcodebuild` test-runner crash, as opposed to a time-limit overrun, also restarts the runner is taken from `xcodebuild`'s message only; the ungated-`.timeLimit` rule holds for this Xcode only (ADR-041 amendment). Added at the level-3 merge (ADR-052; `docs/adr-drafts/c.md` "Not verified here"): the legacy rename on a device (Caches → Application Support on one APFS volume) and a legacy root on another volume (dropped, C-14); `LiveLegacyDefaults`; Unicode normalisation or case differences between legacy path components and on-disk names (a mismatch drops the file); phase 3's durable commit before the `UserDefaults` key removal, which `FakeFileSystem` cannot tell from an ordered one (`.barrier` and `.full` are identical there, OI-2: shown by code reading only, and WP-F must not rely on a fake test to prove it); the legacy overlap guard's identity comparisons, resolved-identity walks and error mapping on a device; deep verification's incarnation check against a real rename-over on APFS (inode reuse could hide a same-size replacement); the kept set across a crash (in memory by design, OI-11); phase 3's mutation-serial guard, which relies on WP-B bumping `commits.mutationSerial` in every persisted change (read, not audited across every WP-B write); OI-8's allocation measurements (macOS 27 and the iOS 27 simulator only); C-22's actor holds, `apply`'s chunked eviction (c-8) and a purge's wait for another reaper's deletion on a device; the holders-scan prefilter's assumption that the first hash word spreads evenly (skew costs time only). Added at the level-4 merge (ADR-053; `docs/adr-drafts/f.md` F-14 and the residue bullets of F-20…F-42, by reference): OI-2, `FakeFileSystem`'s barrier equals a full sync, so the engine's durability ordering (a publication acknowledged only after the store's commit; reconcile committed before the first transfer) is verified against that model only; device-only behaviour: real background `URLSession` relaunches, real Apple-hosted packs (R-APL-3 runs end to end over WP-F's `FakePackBackend` only), real `NWPathMonitor`, thermal and Low Power Mode signals; P-11's speedup as a lower bound of the steady state, because its timed sync overlaps the launch run (F-12); the binding-ledger windows (the resolve-registration and prefetch-join checks have no deterministic probe and cost one job and one read per id, never a download; the one-await window after the last presence read stays open for `recordIntegrityFailure` and the pre-transfer read; a 404 job's waiters for an id bound during `recordFailure`'s await are answered `notFound` while the store serves it; the ledger's invalidation after a throwing store mutation is shown only for a failed index commit; `followStoreView` and `ledgerForgets` are pinned at function level only; a holder gained between `blobHolders` and a quarantine is not announced or healed); the space interleavings (the real-priority form of two committed applies followed out of order, which a seam stands in for; WP-B's unscheduled reclaim after an `apply` that threw, not reproduced; a purge's reaper against the engine's alarm on a busy actor, which a store clock running ahead stands in for; two alarm runs whose store work overlaps, unpinned; bytes reclaimed by maintenance re-checked at the next deadline or read, by design; a settlement read racing a newly armed store deadline, unpinned; a manifest copy changing between two join batches, uncovered); C2 at scale (the first turn of each re-admission event, retries that fall due together in one alarm turn and shutdown are linear in one turn and not measured at 10,000; `registerGroup`'s registering turn at 10,000 members was measured at the level-5 merge, 4.27–4.40 ms, of which the de-duplication and sort 3.44–3.51 ms, by a release-mode probe on macOS 27 at a load of about 4.9, not a device, ADR-054; the 10,000-id prune announcement takes about 3.5 ms and is estimated at about 35 ms at `maxEntries`, not measured); test fidelity (R-SYN-15's join-before-close order has no engine-side assertion; a completion racing the engine's cancel of a kept drained file is shown by reading only; a 404 whose presence reads overlap shutdown and a transfer request after shutdown began are hardened, not produced; the harness priority floor no longer reproduces production's utility/default QoS gap; the model tests have no liveness phase; the gate model's outcome kinds were not extended; condition observers can see a half-started run inside `startRun`, L4-R24(1); whether release builds copy the blob table in `gainReferenceInPlace`, L4-R24(6)). Added at the level-5 merge (ADR-054; `docs/adr-drafts/g.md` "Not verified here", by reference; L5-R23): R-NET-3's run-time half, a background-events hook the app attested (`BackgroundTransferPolicy.enabled`) but did not wire, is neither detected nor tested (under L5-R5 the attestation is what "wired" means in Stage 2; device test plan; Stage 3 may revisit it only with a detection that cannot misfire on an early finish); a real `rootVanished` on a device (iOS purging a Caches root while the app runs) and the reopen there; UIKit's background-events hook end to end (the facade test calls `handleBackgroundEvents` directly); the live `MainBundleLookup` against an app bundle with resources; P-8 and P-9 on a device, and the benchmarks on the iOS Simulator (built, not run, ADR-051); the interleavings the untested lines above list (L5-R9), among them a reopen landing between two hops of one facade call (the background-events hand-off, the diagnostic report's three reads) and a stream's registration overlapping the client's shutdown (shown by reading only); a root that vanishes between `withAsset`'s read of `unavailableReason` and its lease (it leases from the vanished store, a read accepted under L5-R4), and a call that succeeds on a replaced root no commit has detected yet, which does not reopen until a failure that can stem from the file system, or a commit, detects the root; a root check whose stat itself fails (it records nothing; shown by reading only) and the check's cost on a device (one `lstat` on the metadata executor per qualifying failure); the engine's pending claims: a claimed-only job's failure path (shown by reading only), the claims' and L5-R15's C2 figures (release-mode probes on macOS 27 at a load of about 4.9–5.5, never committed, not a device), the "no give-up after a running join's read" invariant (argued, not tested) and the merge of a large give-up into a later large release (no test); R-CAR-5's lost update (a reopen between the engine's answer and the record of a scope) was never reproduced, and the record-first order removes that window by construction (G-3). Added at the level-5 WP-K merge (ADR-055; `docs/adr-drafts/k.md` "Not verified here", by reference; L5K-R28): the shims at run time below the iOS 17 test-bundle target (they ran on the iOS 27 simulator and macOS 27 only; their iOS 15 compile is the `swiftc -typecheck -target arm64-apple-ios15.0` run recorded at `409db9a`, after which only their doc comments changed); the live provider and monitor (`NetworkAvailabilityMonitor.shared` over the live reachability conformer, `StaticManifestCDNProvider` against a real origin and the 403 a keyless client gets there, `defaultAppVersion()` inside an app bundle, `BackgroundAssetsCDNProvider` on a device); `CDNLocalStore(baseDirectory:)`'s placement and migration over the live file system in an app container; Q-10, the adoption inside ZTronCarouselCore (ADR-006: `MIGRATION.md` describes it, and nothing outside this repository was built or written); the migration window's length on a device (K-15: how long migration takes over a real prior-art tree, hence how long the lookups miss its files) and the device cost of the close-out's extra reads, re-arms and installs (L5K-R1's C2 figures come from a release-mode probe on macOS 27; no benchmark, L5K-R11); the main actor's enqueue order (`legacyStoreAdoptsThePriorArtFiles`'s determinism argument); one adoption per prior-art file with the live executors and on a device (L5K-R13); L5K-R16's crossed deadline answers under production priorities (reproduced with a seam); the L5K-R21 tests' gated planner, which blocks one cooperative thread for a few actor hops; how often a device makes a batch's install wait at the main actor long enough for an adopt-on-demand to end during it (L5K-C4-00); the size on a device of the lag L5K-R24 keeps at the other answer sites (P21-COMMIT, P21-ENSURE-MIG and K4-COMMITPROMO ran on macOS 27 only); and, found at the merge and not in the draft, the shims across a facade reopen after a replaced root (ADR-054's WP-K note: no shim test or `MIGRATION.md` passage covers the lookups' `nil` between the mirror reset and the replacement's first install, or the coordinator's subscription across a reopen).

## 15. Hygiene items for the human (outside this repository)

Nothing in this round wrote outside the repository on purpose, and this package must never do so. Two items need the owner, because CLAUDE.md forbids agents to delete outside the repository:
1. **`CFNetworkDownload_*.tmp` partials** left by earlier Stage 1 probes (download tasks, the defect ADR-019 removes): 762 files in the per-user temp directory at the time of this round (about 794 MiB per the transport-2 review). Remove with `rm "$(getconf DARWIN_USER_TEMP_DIR)"CFNetworkDownload_*.tmp`.
2. **Two xcodebuild error result bundles** created by this round's contract author when two mistyped `xcrun --sdk` invocations failed (the SDK name and target were passed as one word): `/var/folders/7y/s6b_jp997bl6j5yw9d17lvfm0000gn/T/ResultBundle_2026-04-10_11-28-0015.xcresult` and `…-0016.xcresult`. Remove with `rm -rf /var/folders/7y/s6b_jp997bl6j5yw9d17lvfm0000gn/T/ResultBundle_2026-04-10_11-28-001{5,6}.xcresult`. Every later command of this round set `TMPDIR` and the Swift module cache to `.build-probe-cfix1/` inside the repository.

Toolchain side effects of the mandated harness commands themselves (SwiftPM and Xcode caches under `~/Library`) are outside this package's control and are not changed by this round.

Contract-fix round 2 wrote nothing outside the repository on purpose: `xcodebuild` and the iOS typecheck ran with `TMPDIR` and the module cache under `.build-probe-cfix2/`, builds used `.build-cfix2`. The test `R-OBS-1: log lines carry…` emitted one `.debug` line through `os.Logger`; the unified log keeps debug lines in memory only by default.

Contract-fix round 3 wrote nothing outside the repository on purpose: builds and tests used `--scratch-path .build-cfix3`, `xcodebuild` used `-derivedDataPath .build-cfix3/xcode`, and every command ran with `TMPDIR` and the Clang module cache under `.build-cfix3/`. Since this round no test writes to the unified log (the R-OBS-1 test routes through `TapOnlyLogSink`, ADR-047).

Contract-fix round 4 wrote nothing outside the repository on purpose: builds and tests used `--scratch-path .build-cfix4`, `xcodebuild` used `-derivedDataPath .build-cfix4/xcode`, and every command ran with `TMPDIR` and the Clang module cache under `.build-cfix4/`. The new package scans only read files inside the repository; the temporary probe file of §13.4 issue 1 was created and deleted inside `Sources/`.

Since the iOS Simulator test build (ADR-051), each agent's `xcodebuild test` installs the test host and bundles into its own named simulator's device container under `~/Library/Developer/CoreSimulator` and writes there while the tests run (owner-accepted 2026-10-09, the one write outside this repository). Agents use one named simulator each, never the owner's booted one, and shut it down afterwards (`xcrun simctl shutdown <UDID>`); `xcodebuild` always gets `-derivedDataPath` inside the repository.
